Second transcription's note: Every 'STOP, you can't take the credit' sets this to zero. The tool refuses instead of computing these cases: Form 8862 (household.credit_disallowed); Form 2555 (sa.excluded_income); nonresident alien and passive activity (scope.other_income); Schedule E and rental of personal property (scope.schedule1); Form 8814 (scope.other_line16); Form 4797 filers with investment income over $11,950 (sd.other_forms, which refuses whenever answered yes); and a qualifying child who is claimed by more than one person, is married, or falls under the divorced or separated parents rule (eic.h_dep_screen).

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 40, Step 1, question 1: "If, in 2025: 3 or more children who have valid SSNs lived with you, is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than $61,555 ($68,675 if married filing jointly)? ... No. STOP You can't take the credit." i1040gi p. 40, Step 2, question 2: "Is your investment income more than $11,950?" Triage executable-triage-v2 (triage/eic.json; 1,111 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 136 partial records; 1,747 comparisons): 1,630 of 1,747 agree; no value difference; 40 candidate-unknown (8 on completed returns), 65 candidate-refuses (all on refused returns), 9 project-unknown, 3 project-refuses. The candidate-unknown come from its eic.h_s1q1, which compares line 11b with a limit chosen by the count of qualifying children with valid SSNs (eic.h_limit), so it needs each dependent's answers (dependent.us_half and the rest) even when line 11b is at or above the largest limit and the question is No whatever the count; the project tests that limit first (eic.below_top) and the interview does not ask those answers there. A candidate that needs answers the source does not need to determine the result is a model error (README), as in round 3. Witnesses: classm.f4952.1 (head of household, line 11b $388,706.00) project $0.00, candidate unknown (dependent[dep_c].us_half); classm.f4952.2 (incomplete) likewise. The candidate-refuses are its declared gates (eic.h_dep_screen: a child who is the qualifying child of another person, married, or under the divorced-parents rule; sa.excluded_income; scope.other_line16) on returns the project also refuses (classm.tcw.10, classm.carloan.1, classm.retirement.3, classm.s179.3): scope. The 3 project-refuses are cited gates (i8889 HSA screen, retirement special exceptions, sa.dependent at i1040gi p. 42, Step 4, question 6 on stage9.eic.refuse.dependent_of_another): scope. The 9 project-unknown (stage14.ho.gross_limit, audit.sa.negative_agi_medical) withhold f1040.tax_exempt_interest, part of the Step 2 investment income the interview asks of every filer; the candidate reaches $0 only because its eligibility test fails first (representation). Second transcription's note: Read literally, a No to question 6 always says to check the box. The Special rule for separated spouses applies only to married people not filing jointly, so this reading checks it only on the married MFS/HOH path (question 3 Yes) that reached question 6 and answered No.

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 41, Step 3, question 6: "No. Check the box in the Dependents section that discusses the special rule for separated spouses"; p. 44, Special rule for separated spouses: "If you meet these requirements, make sure you check the box in the Dependents section that discusses the special rule for separated spouses". Triage executable-triage-v2 (triage/eic.json; 1,111 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 136 partial records; 1,747 comparisons): 1,702 of 1,747 agree; 3 candidate-unknown (1 on a completed return), 35 candidate-refuses, 4 project-refuses, 3 value. The candidate-unknown come from its eic.h_s1q1, which compares line 11b with a limit chosen by the count of qualifying children with valid SSNs (eic.h_limit), so it needs each dependent's answers (dependent.us_half and the rest) even when line 11b is at or above the largest limit and the question is No whatever the count; the project tests that limit first (eic.below_top) and the interview does not ask those answers there. A candidate that needs answers the source does not need to determine the result is a model error (README), as in round 3. Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 40, Step 1, question 1: "If, in 2025: 3 or more children who have valid SSNs lived with you, is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than $61,555 ($68,675 if married filing jointly)? ... No. STOP You can't take the credit." i1040gi p. 46, Worksheet A: "Use this worksheet only if you answered “No” to Step 5, question 2. Otherwise, use Worksheet B." i1040gi p. 46, Worksheet A, line 6: "This is your earned income credit." Triage executable-triage-v2 (triage/eic.json; 1,111 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 136 partial records; 1,747 comparisons): 1,038 of 1,747 agree; 648 candidate-unknown (264 on completed returns), 22 candidate-shows-skipped (10 on completed returns), 39 candidate-refuses. The candidate figures line 6 on every return. Witnesses: stage13.eic_spouse_no_ssn (completed; Step 1, question 2 STOP) hidden, candidate $649.00; stage9.eic.no_child.over65 (completed; Step 4, question 2 STOP) hidden, candidate $308.00; classm.f2441.2.deepseek candidate unknown (lookup above the table after the Step 1 STOP). The candidate-refuses are on refused returns (scope). No value difference.

## Refusal gates listed by the second transcription

Reported, not judged: the project often refuses before a worksheet is used, on the line that chooses it, which a per-line comparison cannot see.

| line | gates listed | not read by the project's line |
| --- | --- | --- |
| eic.credit | eic.h_dep_screen, household.credit_disallowed, s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, sa.excluded_income, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16, scope.schedule1, sd.other_forms | eic.h_dep_screen |
| eic.check_27c | s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | - |
| eic.clergy | scope.schedule1 | - |
| (Step 5, 1) | scope.schedule1 | - |
| (Step 5, 2) | s1.line8_other, scope.other_income | s1.line8_other |
| (Step 5, 3) | s1.line8_other, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | s1.line8_other |
| (Step 5, 4) | s8812.combat_pay | - |
| (Step 5, 5) | s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | s1.line8_other |
| 4b | s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | s1.line8_other |
| 6 (eic.wsb_6) | s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | s1.line8_other |
| 1 | s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | s1.line8_other |
| 2 | s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | s1.line8_other |
| 4 | s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | - |
| 5 | s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | - |
| 6 (eic.line6) | s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | - |

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: eic.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero (a blank line or no credit)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}

  - id: eic.step5_line1
    printed: "(Step 5, 1)"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 1z"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line1z}
    refuses: [scope.schedule1]
    note: "The IRS text replaces line 1z here for clergy and church employees (Step 5, question 1). The tool does not cover clergy or church employee income; scope.schedule1 screens it out, so the tool computes only the plain line 1z case."

  - id: eic.step5_line2
    printed: "(Step 5, 2)"
    label: "Enter the Medicaid waiver payment amounts excluded from income on Schedule 1, line 8s, unless you choose to include these amounts in earned income, in which case enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    refuses: [scope.other_income, s1.line8_other]
    note: "Medicaid waiver payments and the Schedule 1 line 8s exclusion are outside the tool (scope.other_income, s1.line8_other). Within scope, nothing is excluded on line 8s, so this line is zero."

  - id: eic.step5_line3
    printed: "(Step 5, 3)"
    label: "Subtract line 2 from line 1"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: eic.step5_line1, take: eic.step5_line2}

  - id: eic.step5_line4
    printed: "(Step 5, 4)"
    label: "Enter all of your nontaxable combat pay if you elect to include it in earned income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    refuses: [s8812.combat_pay]
    note: "The tool does not cover nontaxable combat pay; s8812.combat_pay refuses it. Within scope, no combat pay is elected, so this line is zero."

  - id: eic.earned_income
    printed: "(Step 5, 5)"
    label: "Add lines 3 and 4. This is your earned income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [eic.step5_line3, eic.step5_line4]}

  - id: eic.self_employed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 5, question 2: Were you self-employed at any time in 2025, or are you filing Schedule SE because you were a member of the clergy or you had church employee income, or are you filing Schedule C as a statutory employee?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [s1.has_sc, {const: true}]}
    note: "Clergy, church employee and statutory employee cases are screened out elsewhere (scope.schedule1, sc.statutory, person.se_screen). Within scope, self-employment means filing a Schedule C, which is s1.has_sc."

  - id: eic.clergy
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 27b / Step 5, question 1: minister, member of a religious order or Christian Science practitioner filing Schedule SE with an amount also reported on line 1z (or church employee income)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [{const: true}, {const: false}]}
    refuses: [scope.schedule1]
    note: "The pages do not say whether this interface means the line 27b box or the Step 5 question 1 answer. Either way, clergy and church employee income are outside the tool (scope.schedule1, person.se_screen), so within scope this is always false."

  - id: eic.h_wsb_1e
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B, line 1e: Schedule SE, Part I, line 3 plus lines 4b and 5a, minus line 13 (you and your spouse combined)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: person, field: person.eic_part1, where: {op: test, eq: [person.files_se, {const: true}]}}
    note: "The project's per-person fields stand in for Worksheet B Part 1 (person.eic_part1) and for the Schedule SE filing test (person.files_se). I could not check their formulas. Schedule SE lines 4b (optional method) and 5a (church employee income) are refused elsewhere."

  - id: eic.h_wsb_2c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B, line 2c: net profit or (loss) from Schedule C, line 31, for self-employed people not required to file Schedule SE"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: person, field: person.sc_total, where: {op: test, eq: [person.files_se, {const: false}]}}
    note: "Farm and partnership amounts (line 2a) and statutory employee income (Part 3) are outside the tool. The project's per-person total of Schedule C line 31 (person.sc_total) is used here."

  - id: eic.wsb_4b
    printed: "4b"
    label: "Combine lines 1e, 2c, 3, and 4a. This is your total earned income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [eic.h_wsb_1e, eic.h_wsb_2c, eic.earned_income]}
    note: "Line 4a is the Step 5 earned income. Line 3 (statutory employees) is out of scope, so it is zero."

  - id: eic.wsb_6
    printed: "6 (eic.wsb_6)"
    label: "Enter your total earned income from Part 4, line 4b"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: eic.wsb_4b, take: {const: 0}}
    note: "If line 4b is zero or less, the STOP applies and the credit is zero (see eic.credit). This line carries 4b floored at zero."

  - id: eic.h_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Married filing jointly"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}

  - id: eic.h_qc_ssn_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of qualifying children who have valid SSNs"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: dependent, field: dependent.one, where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn_child, {const: true}]}}

  - id: eic.h_qc_any_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of children who meet the conditions to be your qualifying child for the EIC (with or without a valid SSN)"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: dependent, field: dependent.one, where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}}

  - id: eic.h_has_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3, question 1: at least one child who meets the conditions to be your qualifying child"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [eic.h_qc_any_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}

  - id: eic.h_dep_screen_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Children who could be the qualifying child of another person, who are married, or to whom the divorced or separated parents rule applies"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [dependent.qc_relationship, {const: true}]}
          - op: test
            any:
              - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_claimant, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [dependent.married, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [dependent.divorced_rule, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_dep_screen
    printed: "(gate)"
    label: "Qualifying child of more than one person, married child, or children of divorced or separated parents (see Pub. 596)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [eic.h_dep_screen_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}

  - id: eic.h_l0o
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, no children: $19,104"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit0.other}
  - id: eic.h_l0j
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, no children, married filing jointly: $26,214"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit0.joint}
  - id: eic.h_l1o
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, 1 child: $50,434"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit1.other}
  - id: eic.h_l1j
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, 1 child, married filing jointly: $57,554"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit1.joint}
  - id: eic.h_l2o
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, 2 children: $57,310"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit2.other}
  - id: eic.h_l2j
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, 2 children, married filing jointly: $64,430"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit2.joint}
  - id: eic.h_l3o
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, 3 or more children: $61,555"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit3.other}
  - id: eic.h_l3j
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, 3 or more children, married filing jointly: $68,675"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit3.joint}

  - id: eic.h_limit0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit for no children, by filing status"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}, then: eic.h_l0j}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_l0o}
  - id: eic.h_limit1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit for 1 child, by filing status"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}, then: eic.h_l1j}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_l1o}
  - id: eic.h_limit2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit for 2 children, by filing status"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}, then: eic.h_l2j}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_l2o}
  - id: eic.h_limit3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit for 3 or more children, by filing status"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}, then: eic.h_l3j}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_l3o}

  - id: eic.h_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Income limit for the number of qualifying children who have valid SSNs (Step 1, question 1; Step 5, question 3; Worksheet B, line 5)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_limit0}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_limit1}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 2, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_limit2}
        - {guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 3, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_limit3}

  - id: eic.h_s1q1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1, question 1: is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than the limit?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, eic.h_limit]}

  - id: eic.h_s1q2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1, question 2: do you, and your spouse if filing a joint return, have an SSN that allows you to work and is valid for EIC purposes?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [household.all_eic_ssn, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_7a_pos
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 1040, line 7a (if line 7a is a loss, enter -0-)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1040.line7a, take: {const: 0}}

  - id: eic.h_investment
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 2, question 1: investment income (lines 2a + 2b + 3b + 7a)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line2a, f1040.line2b, f1040.line3b, eic.h_7a_pos]}

  - id: eic.h_s2_pass
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 2, question 2: investment income is not more than $11,950"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, le: [eic.h_investment, {param: eic.investment_limit}]}

  - id: eic.h_mm
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Married taxpayer whose filing status is married filing separately or head of household"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [household.married, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "hoh"}]}
    note: "household.married counts a married person considered unmarried for head of household, which matches 'married taxpayer whose filing status is ... head of household'."

  - id: eic.h_s3_q5_stop
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3, question 5 STOP: same principal residence for the last 6 months of 2025 and not legally separated and living apart"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mm, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.same_home, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.legally_separated, {const: false}]}

  - id: eic.h_s3_box
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3, question 6 STOP: you could be a qualifying child of another person (check the box on line 27c)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s3_q5_stop, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_s3_pass
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3 completed with a qualifying child: go to Step 5"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s3_q5_stop, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}

  - id: eic.separated_box
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Dependents section box for the special rule for separated spouses (Step 3, question 6, answered No)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s1q1, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s1q2, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s2_pass, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s3_pass, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mm, {const: true}]}
    note: "Read literally, a No to question 6 always says to check the box. The Special rule for separated spouses applies only to married people not filing jointly, so this reading checks it only on the married MFS/HOH path (question 3 Yes) that reached question 6 and answered No."

  - id: eic.h_age_tp_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Taxpayer born after December 31, 1960, and before January 2, 2001"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: person
      field: person.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [person.role, {const: "taxpayer"}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [person.born_before_2001, {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [person.born_after_1960, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_age_all_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You or your spouse born after December 31, 1960, and before January 2, 2001"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: person
      field: person.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [person.born_before_2001, {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [person.born_after_1960, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_age_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4, question 2: were you, or your spouse if filing a joint return, at least age 25 but under age 65 at the end of 2025?"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [eic.h_age_all_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [eic.h_age_tp_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
    note: "The Pub. 596 rule for a spouse or filer who died in 2025 is not encoded. The birth-date test printed in the question is used."

  - id: eic.h_s4_box
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4 STOP with the line 27c box: main home not in the United States (question 3), or you could be a qualifying child of another person (question 5)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mm, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_age_ok, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: false}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_s4_pass
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4 completed without a qualifying child: go to Step 5"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mm, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_age_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [sa.dependent, {const: false}]}
    note: "Question 6 (can you be claimed as a dependent) uses sa.dependent. That input also covers dual-status aliens, who are outside the tool anyway (scope.other_income)."

  - id: eic.h_s5_pass
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 5, question 3 / Worksheet B, line 5: earned income (or line 4b, which must be more than zero) is less than the limit"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.earned_income, eic.h_limit]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [eic.wsb_4b, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.wsb_4b, eic.h_limit]}

  - id: eic.h_s5_box
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B STOP with the line 27c box: line 4b is zero or less, or line 4b is not less than the limit"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, le: [eic.wsb_4b, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, ge: [eic.wsb_4b, eic.h_limit]}

  - id: eic.line1
    printed: "1"
    label: "Enter your earned income from Step 5"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: false}]}, then: eic.earned_income}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}, then: eic.wsb_6}
    note: "Worksheet B lines 6 to 11 repeat Worksheet A lines 1 to 6 with the same lookups, so they are encoded once here. For self-employed filers (Worksheet B), line 1 takes Worksheet B line 6."

  - id: eic.h_t0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table amount for line 1, no qualifying children"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_t1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table amount for line 1, one qualifying child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_t2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table amount for line 1, two qualifying children"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_t3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table amount for line 1, three or more qualifying children"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_line2_table
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 1 in the column for the number of qualifying children who have valid SSNs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_t0}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_t1}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 2, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_t2}
        - {guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 3, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_t3}

  - id: eic.line2
    printed: "2"
    label: "Look up the amount on line 1 above in the EIC Table to find the credit. Be sure you use the correct column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children you have who have valid SSNs. Enter the credit here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, lt: [eic.line1, {param: eic.table_start}]}, then: eic.h_zero}
        - {guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.line1, {param: eic.table_start}]}, then: eic.h_line2_table}
    note: "The table starts at $1. An amount below $1 has no table row; this reading treats it as a zero credit, which is the line 2 STOP."

  - id: eic.line3
    printed: "3"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line11b}

  - id: eic.line4
    printed: "4"
    label: "Are the amounts on lines 3 and 1 the same?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.line3, eic.line1]}

  - id: eic.h_th0o
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold, no qualifying children: $10,620"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold0.other}
  - id: eic.h_th0j
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold, no qualifying children, married filing jointly: $17,730"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold0.joint}
  - id: eic.h_th1o
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold, 1 or more qualifying children: $23,350"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold1.other}
  - id: eic.h_th1j
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold, 1 or more qualifying children, married filing jointly: $30,470"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold1.joint}

  - id: eic.h_th0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold, no qualifying children, by filing status"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}, then: eic.h_th0j}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_th0o}
  - id: eic.h_th1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold, 1 or more qualifying children, by filing status"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}, then: eic.h_th1j}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_th1o}
  - id: eic.h_threshold
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold for the number of qualifying children who have valid SSNs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_th0}
        - {guard: {op: test, gt: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_th1}

  - id: eic.h_line5_needed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 4 is No and line 3 is not less than the threshold (look up line 3)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, ge: [eic.line3, eic.h_threshold]}

  - id: eic.h_a0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table amount for line 3, no qualifying children"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_a1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table amount for line 3, one qualifying child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_a2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table amount for line 3, two qualifying children"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_a3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table amount for line 3, three or more qualifying children"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_line5_table
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 3 in the column for the number of qualifying children who have valid SSNs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_a0}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_a1}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 2, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_a2}
        - {guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_ssn_count, {const: 3, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_a3}

  - id: eic.line5
    printed: "5"
    label: "If line 3 is not less than the threshold, look up the amount on line 3 in the EIC Table to find the credit. Enter the credit here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line5_needed, {const: true}]}, then: eic.h_line5_table}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line5_needed, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_zero}
    note: "When the worksheet says to skip line 5 or leave it blank, this encoding enters zero. Line 6 does not read line 5 in that case."

  - id: eic.h_line6_smaller
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Look at the amounts on lines 5 and 2. Then, enter the smaller amount on line 6."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [eic.line5, eic.line2]}

  - id: eic.line6
    printed: "6 (eic.line6)"
    label: "This is your earned income credit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line5_needed, {const: true}]}, then: eic.h_line6_smaller}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line5_needed, {const: false}]}, then: eic.line2}

  - id: eic.h_eligible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Steps 1 through 5 passed without a STOP"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s1q1, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s1q2, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s2_pass, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s3_pass, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s4_pass, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s5_pass, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.credit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income credit (Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 27a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eligible, {const: true}]}, then: eic.line6}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eligible, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_zero}
    refuses: [household.credit_disallowed, sa.excluded_income, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1, scope.other_line16, sd.other_forms, eic.h_dep_screen]
    note: "Every 'STOP, you can't take the credit' sets this to zero. The tool refuses instead of computing these cases: Form 8862 (household.credit_disallowed); Form 2555 (sa.excluded_income); nonresident alien and passive activity (scope.other_income); Schedule E and rental of personal property (scope.schedule1); Form 8814 (scope.other_line16); Form 4797 filers with investment income over $11,950 (sd.other_forms, which refuses whenever answered yes); and a qualifying child who is claimed by more than one person, is married, or falls under the divorced or separated parents rule (eic.h_dep_screen)."

  - id: eic.check_27c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box on line 27c (instructed to check it in the line 27a instructions)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s1q1, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s1q2, {const: false}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s1q1, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s1q2, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s2_pass, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s3_box, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s4_box, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s1q1, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s1q2, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s2_pass, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s3_pass, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s4_pass, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s5_box, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eligible, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.line2, {const: 0}]}
    note: "The box is checked only for a STOP that tells you to check it and that is reached before any other STOP: Step 1 question 2; Step 3 question 6; Step 4 questions 3 and 5; Worksheet B line 4b of zero or less or line 5 No; and a line 2 (Worksheet B line 7) of zero. The tool has no input for choosing not to claim the credit."
```
```
